HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B002","root_000348/B004","root_001283/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_036675a55d3c48fb8c08","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:2:3\",\"root_001283\",[\"B003\"]],[\"31:2:4\",\"root_000348\",[\"B002\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kitabın bağlayıcı biçimde yazılmış olması, onu iddiaları ayırt edebilen sağlam bir ölçüt haline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001283/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_23fa7c5b06d54226d5e5","text":"1. **A joined text that restrains disorder.** Lexical evidence: `ك ت ب B001` is joining one thing to another, `ك ت ب B002` is joined letters in a written book, and `ح ك م B001` is restraint that turns corruption back toward rightness. Structural cue: the compact genitive construction joins signs, book, and wisdom; 31:4-5 then display ordered practice, whereas 31:6-7 display diversion and refusal. Assigned role: the book's joined units form a restraining architecture. Abductive move (inferred): textual joining enables ethical restraint because ordered signs can coordinate otherwise scattered action. Reading change: `ٱلْكِتَٰبِ ٱلْحَكِيمِ` reads as an operative ordering instrument, not only a book possessing abstract wisdom.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001283/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4b517f0b819dc2c172e4","text":"2. **A text made firm enough to adjudicate.** **ك ت ب B003** supplies binding inscription, while **ح ك م B002** and **B004** supply adjudication and decisive perfecting. Their adjacency makes firmness the condition under which the book can distinguish claims. The inferred direction is inscription → stable criterion. This activates a juridical reading alongside the epistemic one: these signs constitute an enforceable standard rather than loose counsel.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000074/B003","root_000348/B001","root_001283/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4c56d617fbfbafbaa059","text":"1. **Signs joined into a restraining instrument.** **ء ي ي B003** gives the visible sign; **ك ت ب B001–B002** gives joining and joined letters; **ح ك م B001** gives restraint that turns something back toward rightness. The construct sequence moves from discrete signs to joined book to its governing quality. I infer that ordered joining enables the signs to check misdirection. The “wise book” therefore reads not only as a repository of wisdom but as an assembled corrective mechanism.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_758452489b93e6e825c4","text":"- 31:7 was the decisive later surprise: the same signs became recited sequence and met a simulated ear-load. It sharpened the book's wisdom into an ordering force whose failure point is active reception, not lack of textual or perceptible form.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_82e7e9fc90f251d0cd16","text":"Ayah 31:20’s “illuminating book” sharpened the book as an orienting medium, but 31:27 unexpectedly placed all pens and ink below unexhausted divine words. The book’s joined firmness is therefore not a claim that inscription contains the source exhaustively.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000074/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8725d1ff3715e9aeeb89","text":"2. **Signs that demand both sight and receptive hearing.** Lexical evidence: `ء ي ي B003` is a visible sign or marked unit, while `ح ك م B004` supplies firmness and perfected decisiveness. Structural cue: the same `ءَايَٰت` are recited in 31:7, where the hearer turns away and imagines a weight in his ears. Assigned role: the sign is a stable perceptible marker that can cross from written/visible form into recited/audible sequence. Abductive move (inferred): the later refusal shows that the obstacle lies in reception, not in the signs' lack of perceptibility. Reading change: `ءَايَٰتُ` becomes a multi-modal evidence system whose marked clarity can still be actively resisted.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000074/B003","root_000348/B001","root_001283/B001","root_001283/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_951c43d72f23b049254a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:2:2\",\"root_000074\",[\"B003\"]],[\"31:2:3\",\"root_001283\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"31:2:4\",\"root_000348\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ayetlerin kitapta düzenli biçimde birleştirilmesi, dağınık davranışı doğruya çeviren işleyen bir düzeltme düzeni oluşturur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000074/B003","root_000348/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e653fd301d1aa46fd377","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:2:2\",\"root_000074\",[\"B003\"]],[\"31:2:4\",\"root_000348\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bu ayetler, yazıda görülen ve okunduğunda işitmeye açılan, fakat insanın direnerek alımlanmasını kesebildiği çok yönlü kanıtlardır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
